[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +/* Changed words of TIM15 (bit i), RCC words in bits 30/31. */
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t m4_diff(bool running)
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t d = 0U;

[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +        if (running && ((i == TST_RI_CNT) || (i == TST_RI_SR)))
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +            continue;
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +        if (s_m4_a.im.v[i] != s_m4_b.im.v[i])
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +            d |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +    d |= (s_m4_a.rcc[0] != s_m4_b.rcc[0]) ? (1UL << 30) : 0U;
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +    d |= (s_m4_a.rcc[1] != s_m4_b.rcc[1]) ? (1UL << 31) : 0U;
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +    return d;
[+   1.684s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +                first = now;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +            last = now;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +            n++;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +            if ((last - first) >= window)
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +                break;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM15);
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    out[0] = ticks;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    out[1] = n;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    out[2] = last - first;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +static void run_t5(volatile uint32_t *r)
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_EnableClock(TIM15);

[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    t5_15(&a, 1000U, &r[0]);
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM15);

[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    b.repetition = 3U;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    t5_15(&b, 4000U, &r[3]);

[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t hclk = HAL_RCC_GetHCLKFreq();

[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T6: TIM15 PWM on PE5/PE6 (step T6_TIM15) -------------------------------------- */
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +static void run_t6(uint32_t arg, volatile uint32_t *r)
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_EnableClock(TIM15);
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    if (arg == TST_T6_OFF)
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        (void)pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_T15C1, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        (void)pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_T15C2, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        r[1] = t14_tim15();
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        r[0] = 0U;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    if (!pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_T15C1, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_TIM15, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN) ||
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        !pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_T15C2, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_TIM15, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN))
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        r[0] = 98U;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    if (arg == TST_T6_CONTROL)
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        /* both channels configured, CC1E = CC2E = 0 (and so MOE = 0): the pins are not
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +           driven by the timer and follow the pull-down */
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM15);
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM15, 0U, 199U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, true, false, false };
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_OcConfig_t o1 = { TIM15, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_OcConfig_t o2 = { TIM15, 2U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        if (st == TIM_BM_OK) { st = TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c); }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        if (st == TIM_BM_OK) { st = TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&o1); }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        if (st == TIM_BM_OK) { st = TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&o2); }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        if (st == TIM_BM_OK) { st = TIM_BM_Start(TIM15); }
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        r[0] = (uint32_t)st;
[+   1.685s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t ch = arg >> 8;
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t k = arg & 0xFFU;
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    if ((ch < 1U) || (ch > 2U) || (k >= (TST_T6_POINTS - 1U)))
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +        r[0] = 97U;
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +    r[0] = (uint32_t)tst_t6_apply(TIM15, ch, &k_tst_t6[k]);
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T10(d): TIM15 captures TRC = ITR0 (TIM1 TRGO, run by CPU1) ---------------------- */
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.686s] [host] diff| +static void run_t10d_arm(volatile uint32_t *r)

[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +   `running`, TIM2's CNT and SR are not compared (it counts on purpose). */
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +static void t1_diff(uint32_t tim[T1_NT], uint32_t *rcc, bool running)
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    *rcc = 0U;
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t j = 0U; j < 6U; j++)
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        if (s_t1_a.rcc[j] != s_t1_b.rcc[j])
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            *rcc |= 1UL << j;
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < T1_NT; k++)
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        tim[k] = 0U;
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < TST_RI_N; i++)
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            if (running && (k == 1U) && ((i == TST_RI_CNT) || (i == TST_RI_SR)))
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +                continue;
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            if (s_t1_a.im[k].v[i] != s_t1_b.im[k].v[i])
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +                tim[k] |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +typedef struct { const char *what; uint8_t expect; uint8_t running; } T1Case_t;
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +static const T1Case_t k_t1[] = {
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    /*  0 */ { "InitTimeBase(NULL)",                                   TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    /*  1 */ { "InitTimeBase(USART3)",                                 TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    /*  2 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM1 + 4)",                               TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    /*  3 */ { "EnableClock(USART3)",                                  TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.693s] [host] diff| +    /*  4 */ { "EnableClock(TIM1 + 4)",                                TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /*  5 */ { "ConfigOutput(TIM2 channel 0)",                         TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /*  6 */ { "ConfigOutput(TIM2 channel 5)",                         TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /*  7 */ { "ConfigOutput(TIM12 channel 3)",                        TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /*  8 */ { "ConfigOutput(TIM7 channel 1): PWM on TIM7",            TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /*  9 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM2 ARR = 0)",                           TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 10 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM4 ARR = 0x10000, 16 bits)",            TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 11 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM2 PSC = 65536)",                       TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 12 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM17 repetition = 0x100, 8-bit RCR)",    TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 13 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM12 center 1)",                         TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 14 */ { "ConfigSlave(TIM4 trigger ITR5)",                       TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 15 */ { "ConfigSlave(TIM2 trigger 19: no ETRF value)",          TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 16 */ { "ConfigSlave(TIM4 trigger 7: ETRF's TS code = ITR7)",   TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 17 */ { "ConfigInput(TIM17 TI1SEL = 0001, reserved)",           TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 18 */ { "2.16.1 ConfigMaster(TIM2 MSM) with OPM, reset+trigger", TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 19 */ { "2.16.1 ConfigSlave(TIM2 reset+trigger) with OPM, MSM", TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 20 */ { "2.16.1 InitTimeBase(TIM2 OPM) with MSM, reset+trigger", TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 21 */ { "ConfigOutput(TIM2 CH1) with CC1E = 1",                 TIM_BM_ERR_BUSY,        0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 22 */ { "ConfigInput(TIM2 CH2) with CC2E = 1",                  TIM_BM_ERR_BUSY,        0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 23 */ { "InitTimeBase(TIM2) with CEN = 1",                      TIM_BM_ERR_BUSY,        1U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 24 */ { "ConfigSlave(TIM2 trigger) with SMS = gated",           TIM_BM_ERR_BUSY,        0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 25 */ { "DutyToCompare(duty 10001)",                            TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 26 */ { "DutyToCompare(100 %, ARR 0xFFFF, 16 bits)",            TIM_BM_ERR_RANGE,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 27 */ { "EnableDmaRequest(TIM4 CC4: no DMAMUX1 request)",       TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 28 */ { "OutputEnable(TIM2 CH1) on an input",                   TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 29 */ { "CaptureEnable(TIM2 CH1) on an output",                 TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 30 */ { "EnableIrq(TIM13 TRIGGER)",                             TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 31 */ { "SetPeriod(TIM2, 0)",                                   TIM_BM_ERR_PARAM,       0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 32 */ { "ConfigMaster(TIM12 OC3REF > MMS 5)",                   TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    /* 33 */ { "ConfigMaster(TIM13: no TRGO)",                         TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED, 0U },
