[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +};
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +#define T1_CASES   (sizeof(k_t1) / sizeof(k_t1[0]))
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +static TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t tb_cfg(TIM_TypeDef *t, uint32_t psc, uint32_t arr, uint32_t mode, bool opm)
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { t, psc, arr, mode, 0U, false, opm, false };
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    return c;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +static TimBm_Status_t t1_case(uint32_t k)
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t tb = tb_cfg(TIM2, 0U, 999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { TIM2, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_IcConfig_t ic = { TIM2, 2U, TIM_BM_IC_DIRECT, TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, 1U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_OK;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t taken = 0U;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t v = 0U;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +#define T1_CALL(expr)  do { t1_snap(&s_t1_b); st = (expr); t1_snap(&s_t1_a); } while (0)
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    switch (k)
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 0:  T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(NULL)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 1:  tb.tim = (TIM_TypeDef *)USART3; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 2:  tb.tim = (TIM_TypeDef *)(TIM1_BASE + 4U); T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 3:  T1_CALL(TIM_BM_EnableClock((TIM_TypeDef *)USART3)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 4:  T1_CALL(TIM_BM_EnableClock((TIM_TypeDef *)(TIM1_BASE + 4U))); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 5:  oc.channel = 0U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 6:  oc.channel = 5U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 7:  oc.tim = TIM12; oc.channel = 3U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 8:  oc.tim = TIM7; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 9:  tb.arr = 0U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 10: tb.tim = TIM4; tb.arr = 0x10000U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 11: tb.psc = 65536U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 12: tb.tim = TIM17; tb.repetition = 0x100U; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 13: tb.tim = TIM12; tb.count_mode = TIM_BM_COUNT_CENTER1; T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 14: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM4, TIM_BM_SMS_TRIGGER, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(5))); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 15: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_TRIGGER, 19U)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 16: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM4, TIM_BM_SMS_TRIGGER, 7U)); break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 17:
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        ic.tim = TIM17; ic.channel = 1U; ic.tisel = 1U;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic));
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 18:
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        tb.one_pulse = true;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb);
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_RESET_TRIGGER, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(0));
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM2, TIM_BM_MMS_RESET, true));
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +    case 19:
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        tb.one_pulse = true;
[+   1.694s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM2, TIM_BM_MMS_RESET, true);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_RESET_TRIGGER, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(0)));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 20:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM2, TIM_BM_MMS_RESET, true);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_RESET_TRIGGER, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(0));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        tb.one_pulse = true;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 21:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_OutputEnable(TIM2, TIM_BM_CH(1));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 22:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_CaptureEnable(TIM2, TIM_BM_CH(2));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 23:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM2);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&tb));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 24:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_GATED, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(0));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_TRIGGER, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(1)));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 25: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_DutyToCompare(199U, 16U, 10001U, &v)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 26: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_DutyToCompare(0xFFFFU, 16U, 10000U, &v)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 27: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_EnableDmaRequest(TIM4, TIM_BM_EV_CC4)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 28:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        ic.channel = 1U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_OutputEnable(TIM2, TIM_BM_CH(1)));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 29:
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        T1_CALL(TIM_BM_CaptureEnable(TIM2, TIM_BM_CH(1)));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 30: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_EnableIrq(TIM13, TIM_BM_EV_TRIGGER)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 31: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_SetPeriod(TIM2, 0U)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 32: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM12, TIM_BM_MMS_OC3REF, false)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 33: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM13, TIM_BM_MMS_UPDATE, false)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 34: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigPwmInput(TIM13, 1U, TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, 1U, 0U, 0U)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 35: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_GATED, TIM_BM_TRG_TI1F_ED)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 36: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_WaitFlags(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, 1000U, NULL)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 37: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_KernelClockFromFields(200000000UL, 8U, 0U, &v)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    case 38: T1_CALL(TIM_BM_EnableIrq(TIM2, 1UL << 5)); break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    default: break;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +#undef T1_CALL
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    (void)taken;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    return st;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static void test_t1(void)
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    uint64_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    uint64_t touched = 0U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < T1_CASES; k++)
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t tm[T1_NT];
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t rcc = 0U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_Status_t st = t1_case(k);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        t1_diff(tm, &rcc, k_t1[k].running != 0U);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        bool same = (rcc == 0U);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t j = 0U; j < T1_NT; j++)
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            same = same && (tm[j] == 0U);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        if (st != (TimBm_Status_t)k_t1[k].expect)
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1ULL << k;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        if (!same)
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            touched |= 1ULL << k;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T1 %lu %s: status %u (expected %u), registers and RCC banks unchanged %u", (unsigned long)k, k_t1[k].what,
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)st, (unsigned)k_t1[k].expect, (unsigned)same);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t j = 0U; j < T1_NT; j++)
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(k_t1_tims[j]);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (touched == 0U),
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            "T1 CM7 %u refusals: wrong status 0x%08lX%08lX, snapshot changed 0x%08lX%08lX", (unsigned)T1_CASES,
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(bad >> 32), (unsigned long)(bad & 0xFFFFFFFFU), (unsigned long)(touched >> 32),
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(touched & 0xFFFFFFFFU));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    /* Sensitivity (audit O1): valid calls change the snapshot, exactly in the expected
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +       words, so an unchanged snapshot above means something. */
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t tm[T1_NT];
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t rcc = 0U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { TIM2, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(&s_t1_b);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_Status_t st1 = TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(&s_t1_a);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    t1_diff(tm, &rcc, false);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t exp_tim2 = (1UL << TST_RI_CCMR1) | (1UL << TST_RI_CCR1);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    const bool others_same = (tm[0] | tm[2] | tm[3] | tm[4] | tm[5] | rcc) == 0U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st1 == TIM_BM_OK) && (tm[1] == exp_tim2) && others_same,
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            "T1 control (O1): ConfigOutput(TIM2 CH1 PWM1 CCR 100 preload) status %u changes TIM2 words 0x%07lX "
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            "(0x%07lX: CCMR1, CCR1), other timers and RCC 0x%lX (0)", (unsigned)st1, (unsigned long)tm[1],
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)exp_tim2, (unsigned long)(tm[0] | tm[2] | tm[3] | tm[4] | tm[5] | rcc));
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM2);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_DisableClock(TIM13);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(&s_t1_b);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_Status_t st2 = TIM_BM_EnableClock(TIM13);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(&s_t1_a);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    t1_diff(tm, &rcc, false);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    const bool tims_same = (tm[0] | tm[1] | tm[2] | tm[3] | tm[4] | tm[5]) == 0U;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st2 == TIM_BM_OK) && (rcc == 0x1U) && tims_same,
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            "T1 control (O1): EnableClock(TIM13) status %u changes RCC words 0x%02lX (0x01: RCC_C1 APB1LENR only), "
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +            "timer words unchanged %u", (unsigned)st2, (unsigned long)rcc, (unsigned)tims_same);
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| + * T2: register images against the hand encodings; on CPU1 also against the raw HAL
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static TIM_HandleTypeDef s_h;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_hal_diff[N_TIMS][TST_T2_CLASSES][TST_RI_N];
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_hal_err;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_hal_cur;
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static const char *const k_cls_name[TST_T2_CLASSES] = { "time base", "output compare", "input capture", "master", "slave" };
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.695s] [host] diff| +static void hal_base(TIM_TypeDef *t, uint32_t psc, uint32_t arr, uint32_t mode, uint32_t rep, bool arpe)
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_cm[5] = { TIM_COUNTERMODE_UP, TIM_COUNTERMODE_DOWN, TIM_COUNTERMODE_CENTERALIGNED1,
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +                                      TIM_COUNTERMODE_CENTERALIGNED2, TIM_COUNTERMODE_CENTERALIGNED3 };
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    memset(&s_h, 0, sizeof(s_h));
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Instance               = t;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Init.Prescaler         = psc;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Init.CounterMode       = k_cm[mode];
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Init.Period            = arr;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Init.ClockDivision     = TIM_CLOCKDIVISION_DIV1;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Init.RepetitionCounter = rep;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    s_h.Init.AutoReloadPreload = arpe ? TIM_AUTORELOAD_PRELOAD_ENABLE : TIM_AUTORELOAD_PRELOAD_DISABLE;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t hal_channel(uint32_t ch)
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    return (ch - 1U) * 4U;     /* TIM_CHANNEL_1..4 = 0x0, 0x4, 0x8, 0xC */
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +/* The same intent through the raw HAL (HAL_TIM_* without the driver), then the bits in
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +   which its image differs from the driver's. */
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +static void hal_hook(const TstT2Cfg_t *c, const TstImg_t *bm)
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_ocm[8] = { TIM_OCMODE_TIMING, TIM_OCMODE_ACTIVE, TIM_OCMODE_INACTIVE, TIM_OCMODE_TOGGLE,
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +                                       TIM_OCMODE_FORCED_INACTIVE, TIM_OCMODE_FORCED_ACTIVE, TIM_OCMODE_PWM1,
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +                                       TIM_OCMODE_PWM2 };
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_sel[4] = { 0U, TIM_ICSELECTION_DIRECTTI, TIM_ICSELECTION_INDIRECTTI, TIM_ICSELECTION_TRC };
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_trg[19] = { TIM_TS_ITR0, TIM_TS_ITR1, TIM_TS_ITR2, TIM_TS_ITR3, TIM_TS_ITR4, TIM_TS_ITR5,
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +                                        TIM_TS_ITR6, TIM_TS_ITR7, TIM_TS_ITR8, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U,
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +                                        TIM_TS_TI1F_ED, TIM_TS_TI1FP1, TIM_TS_TI2FP2 };
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    TIM_TypeDef *t = k_tims[s_hal_cur].tim;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_Caps_t caps;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    (void)tst_hand_caps(t, &caps);
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cmax = (caps.counter_bits == 32U) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0xFFFFUL;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    HAL_StatusTypeDef hs = HAL_OK;
[+   1.696s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(t);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    switch (c->cls)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    case TST_T2_TB:
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hal_base(t, c->tb.psc, c->tb.arr, c->tb.count_mode, c->tb.repetition, c->tb.arr_preload);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hs = c->tb.one_pulse ? HAL_TIM_OnePulse_Init(&s_h, TIM_OPMODE_SINGLE) : HAL_TIM_Base_Init(&s_h);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if (c->tb.urs_overflow_only)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            __HAL_TIM_URS_ENABLE(&s_h);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    case TST_T2_OC:
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        const bool pwm = c->oc.mode >= TIM_BM_OC_PWM1;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        TIM_OC_InitTypeDef oc = { 0 };
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hal_base(t, 0U, cmax, 0U, 0U, false);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hs = pwm ? HAL_TIM_PWM_Init(&s_h) : HAL_TIM_OC_Init(&s_h);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.OCMode       = k_ocm[c->oc.mode];
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.Pulse        = c->oc.compare;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.OCPolarity   = c->oc.active_low ? TIM_OCPOLARITY_LOW : TIM_OCPOLARITY_HIGH;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.OCNPolarity  = TIM_OCNPOLARITY_HIGH;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.OCFastMode   = TIM_OCFAST_DISABLE;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.OCIdleState  = TIM_OCIDLESTATE_RESET;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        oc.OCNIdleState = TIM_OCNIDLESTATE_RESET;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if (hs == HAL_OK)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            hs = pwm ? HAL_TIM_PWM_ConfigChannel(&s_h, &oc, hal_channel(c->oc.channel))
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +                     : HAL_TIM_OC_ConfigChannel(&s_h, &oc, hal_channel(c->oc.channel));
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if ((hs == HAL_OK) && c->oc.preload && !pwm)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            __HAL_TIM_ENABLE_OCxPRELOAD(&s_h, hal_channel(c->oc.channel));
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    case TST_T2_IC:
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        TIM_IC_InitTypeDef ic = { 0 };
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hal_base(t, 0U, cmax, 0U, 0U, false);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hs = HAL_TIM_IC_Init(&s_h);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        ic.ICPolarity  = (c->ic.edge == TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING) ? TIM_ICPOLARITY_RISING
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +                         : ((c->ic.edge == TIM_BM_EDGE_FALLING) ? TIM_ICPOLARITY_FALLING : TIM_ICPOLARITY_BOTHEDGE);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        ic.ICSelection = k_sel[c->ic.selection];
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        ic.ICPrescaler = (c->ic.prescaler == 8U) ? TIM_ICPSC_DIV8 : ((c->ic.prescaler == 4U) ? TIM_ICPSC_DIV4
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +                         : ((c->ic.prescaler == 2U) ? TIM_ICPSC_DIV2 : TIM_ICPSC_DIV1));
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        ic.ICFilter    = c->ic.filter;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if (hs == HAL_OK)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            hs = HAL_TIM_IC_ConfigChannel(&s_h, &ic, hal_channel(c->ic.channel));
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if (hs == HAL_OK)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            hs = HAL_TIMEx_TISelection(&s_h, c->ic.tisel << (8U * (c->ic.channel - 1U)), hal_channel(c->ic.channel));
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    case TST_T2_MS:
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        TIM_MasterConfigTypeDef mc = { 0 };
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hal_base(t, 0U, cmax, 0U, 0U, false);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hs = HAL_TIM_Base_Init(&s_h);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        mc.MasterOutputTrigger  = c->mms << 4;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        mc.MasterOutputTrigger2 = 0U;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        mc.MasterSlaveMode      = c->msm ? TIM_MASTERSLAVEMODE_ENABLE : TIM_MASTERSLAVEMODE_DISABLE;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if (hs == HAL_OK)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            hs = HAL_TIMEx_MasterConfigSynchronization(&s_h, &mc);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    default:   /* TST_T2_SL */
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        static const uint32_t k_sm[9] = { TIM_SLAVEMODE_DISABLE, 0U, 0U, 0U, TIM_SLAVEMODE_RESET, TIM_SLAVEMODE_GATED,
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +                                          TIM_SLAVEMODE_TRIGGER, TIM_SLAVEMODE_EXTERNAL1,
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +                                          TIM_SLAVEMODE_COMBINED_RESETTRIGGER };
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        TIM_SlaveConfigTypeDef sc = { 0 };
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hal_base(t, 0U, cmax, 0U, 0U, false);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        hs = HAL_TIM_Base_Init(&s_h);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        sc.SlaveMode        = k_sm[c->sms];
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        sc.InputTrigger     = k_trg[c->trg];
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        sc.TriggerPolarity  = TIM_TRIGGERPOLARITY_RISING;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        sc.TriggerPrescaler = TIM_TRIGGERPRESCALER_DIV1;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        sc.TriggerFilter    = 0U;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if (hs == HAL_OK)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            hs = HAL_TIM_SlaveConfigSynchro(&s_h, &sc);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    if (hs != HAL_OK)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        s_hal_err++;
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t hal;       /* static: the Status_Task stack is 512 words */
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    tst_img_read(t, &hal);
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < TST_RI_N; i++)
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        if ((i != TST_RI_CNT) && (i != TST_RI_SR))
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +            s_hal_diff[s_hal_cur][c->cls][i] |= hal.v[i] ^ bm->v[i];
[+   1.697s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +static const char *const k_ri_name[TST_RI_N] = {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    "CR1", "CR2", "SMCR", "DIER", "SR", "EGR", "CCMR1", "CCMR2", "CCER", "CNT", "PSC", "ARR", "RCR", "CCR1",
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    "CCR2", "CCR3", "CCR4", "BDTR", "DCR", "DMAR", "res", "CCMR3", "CCR5", "CCR6", "AF1", "AF2", "TISEL",
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +};
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +static void test_t2(void)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    /* The capability table of the driver against the one typed in the test (plan rule 2). */
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t caps_bad = 0U;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < N_ALL; i++)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        TimBm_Caps_t got;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        TimBm_Caps_t exp;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_GetCaps(k_all_tims[i].tim, &got);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        (void)tst_hand_caps(k_all_tims[i].tim, &exp);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        const bool same = (st == TIM_BM_OK) && (got.counter_bits == exp.counter_bits) && (got.channels == exp.channels) &&
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                          (got.itr_mask == exp.itr_mask) && (got.mms_max == exp.mms_max) &&
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                          (got.up_down_center == exp.up_down_center) && (got.repetition == exp.repetition) &&
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                          (got.bdtr == exp.bdtr) && (got.slave_mode == exp.slave_mode) &&
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                          (got.master_mode == exp.master_mode) && (got.tisel_mask[0] == exp.tisel_mask[0]) &&
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                          (got.tisel_mask[1] == exp.tisel_mask[1]) && (got.tisel_mask[2] == exp.tisel_mask[2]) &&
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                          (got.tisel_mask[3] == exp.tisel_mask[3]);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        if (!same)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            caps_bad |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            logw("T2 caps %s: bits %u ch %u itr 0x%03X mms %u udc %u rep %u bdtr %u slave %u master %u tisel %X/%X/%X/%X",
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                k_all_tims[i].name, got.counter_bits, got.channels, got.itr_mask, got.mms_max, got.up_down_center,
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                got.repetition, got.bdtr, got.slave_mode, got.master_mode, got.tisel_mask[0], got.tisel_mask[1],
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                got.tisel_mask[2], got.tisel_mask[3]);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    verdict(caps_bad == 0U, "T2 GetCaps of the 14 instances equals the hand table (RM0399): differing 0x%04lX",
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)caps_bad);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    memset(s_hal_diff, 0, sizeof(s_hal_diff));
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    s_hal_err = 0U;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t total = 0U;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < N_TIMS; i++)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        TstT2Result_t r;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        s_hal_cur = i;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        tst_t2_run(k_tims[i].tim, hal_hook, &r);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        total += r.configs;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        const bool ok = (r.mismatches == 0U) && (r.calls_bad == 0U) && (r.ctrl_detected == 1U) && (r.configs > 0U);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        if (!ok)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        logw("T2 %s: %lu images (time base %lu, OC %lu, IC %lu, master %lu, slave %lu), mismatches %lu, calls not OK %lu, "
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            "altered image flagged %lu%s", k_tims[i].name, (unsigned long)r.configs, (unsigned long)r.per_class[0],
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)r.per_class[1], (unsigned long)r.per_class[2], (unsigned long)r.per_class[3],
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)r.per_class[4], (unsigned long)r.mismatches, (unsigned long)r.calls_bad,
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)r.ctrl_detected, ok ? "" : " <-");
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        if (r.mismatches != 0U)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            logw("T2 %s first mismatch: %s image %lu, %s reads 0x%08lX, hand encoding 0x%08lX", k_tims[i].name,
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                k_cls_name[r.first_cls], (unsigned long)r.first_cfg, k_ri_name[r.first_idx], (unsigned long)r.first_got,
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)r.first_exp);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        (void)t14(k_tims[i].tim, "T2");
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T2 CM7 register images: %lu configurations on 12 instances, instances failing 0x%04lX "
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            "(each control: an expected image altered on purpose is flagged)", (unsigned long)total, (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    /* The raw HAL: every bit in which its image differed from the driver's, per instance,
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +       class and register (the report explains each from stm32h7xx_hal_tim.c). */
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t lines = 0U;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < N_TIMS; i++)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t c = 0U; c < TST_T2_CLASSES; c++)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t w = 0U; w < TST_RI_N; w++)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                if (s_hal_diff[i][c][w] != 0U)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                    logw("T2 HAL %s %s: %s bits 0x%08lX differ from tim_bm", k_tims[i].name, k_cls_name[c], k_ri_name[w],
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                        (unsigned long)s_hal_diff[i][c][w]);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                    lines++;
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +                }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +    logw("T2 HAL raw images: %lu configurations, %lu HAL calls not HAL_OK, %lu differing (instance, class, register) "
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +        "entries listed above", (unsigned long)total, (unsigned long)s_hal_err, (unsigned long)lines);
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| + * T5: time base against CPU1's CYCCNT (2 cycles per timer tick)
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +/* Updates over >= 100 ms of CYCCNT, from the first to the last one. err: against `ticks`
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +   timer ticks per update; ctrl: the same measurement against `ctrl_ticks`. */
[+   1.698s] [host] diff| +static bool t5_measure(TIM_TypeDef *t, const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t *cfg, uint32_t ticks, uint32_t ctrl_ticks,
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +                       const char *what)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(cfg);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t first = 0U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t last = 0U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Start(t);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t window = 100U * CYC_PER_MS;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    while ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (window + (8U * ticks * CYC_PER_TICK) + CYC_PER_MS))
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(t, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t now = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            if (n == 0U)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +                first = now;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            last = now;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            n++;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            if ((last - first) >= window)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +                break;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(t);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = (st == TIM_BM_OK) && (n >= 3U);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    int32_t err = 0;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    int32_t ctrl = 0;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    if (ok)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const int64_t span = (int64_t)(uint32_t)(last - first);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const int64_t exp  = (int64_t)(n - 1U) * ticks * CYC_PER_TICK;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const int64_t expc = (int64_t)(n - 1U) * ctrl_ticks * CYC_PER_TICK;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        err  = (int32_t)(((span - exp) * 1000000) / exp);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        ctrl = (int32_t)(((span - expc) * 1000000) / expc);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        ok = (err >= -100) && (err <= 100) && ((ctrl < -100) || (ctrl > 100));
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ok, "T5 %s %s: %lu updates over %lu cycles, %lu ticks each: %+ld ppm (|e| <= 100); control %lu ticks: %+ld ppm",
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            tim_name(t), what, (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)(last - first), (unsigned long)ticks, (long)err,
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)ctrl_ticks, (long)ctrl);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(t, "T5");
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +static void test_t5(void)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < N_TIMS; i++)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        /* (a) PSC + 1 = 2, ARR + 1 = 500: 1000 ticks; control PSC + 2 */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(k_tims[i].tim, 1U, 499U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        (void)t5_measure(k_tims[i].tim, &c, 1000U, 1500U, "(a) up, PSC=1 ARR=499");
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    TIM_TypeDef *const rcr_tims[4] = { TIM1, TIM8, TIM16, TIM17 };
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        /* (b) RCR = 3: one update every 4 periods */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(rcr_tims[i], 1U, 499U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        c.repetition = 3U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        (void)t5_measure(rcr_tims[i], &c, 4000U, 1000U, "(b) RCR=3");
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    TIM_TypeDef *const w32[2] = { TIM2, TIM5 };
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 2U; i++)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        /* (c) ARR + 1 = 0x20000: a 16-bit counter would give half the period */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(w32[i], 0U, 0x1FFFFU, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        (void)t5_measure(w32[i], &c, 0x20000U, 0x10000U, "(c) 32 bits, ARR=0x1FFFF");
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    TIM_TypeDef *const dc[2] = { TIM1, TIM2 };
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 2U; i++)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        /* (d) down: (ARR + 1)(PSC + 1); center 1: the counter period is 2 ARR ticks. TIM1
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +           with RCR = 1 updates once per period; TIM2 (no RCR) at both turns, ARR apart. */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t d = tb_cfg(dc[i], 1U, 499U, TIM_BM_COUNT_DOWN, false);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        (void)t5_measure(dc[i], &d, 1000U, 1500U, "(d) down");
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(dc[i], 1U, 500U, TIM_BM_COUNT_CENTER1, false);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        c.repetition = (dc[i] == TIM1) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t per_update = (dc[i] == TIM1) ? (2U * 500U * 2U) : (500U * 2U);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t edge_like  = (dc[i] == TIM1) ? (2U * 501U * 2U) : (501U * 2U);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        (void)t5_measure(dc[i], &c, per_update, edge_like,
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +                         (dc[i] == TIM1) ? "(d) center 1, ARR=500, RCR=1: period 2 ARR" : "(d) center 1, ARR=500: update every ARR, period 2 ARR");
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    /* (e) the kernel clock of every instance from table 59 */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t hclk = HAL_RCC_GetHCLKFreq();
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < N_ALL; i++)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t hz = 0U;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_GetKernelClockHz(k_all_tims[i].tim, hclk, &hz);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        if ((st != TIM_BM_OK) || (hz != T_CLK_HZ))
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            logw("T5(e) %s: status %u, %lu Hz", k_all_tims[i].name, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)hz);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T5(e) GetKernelClockHz(hclk1 = %lu Hz) = %lu Hz on all 14 instances (D2PPRE1=%lu D2PPRE2=%lu "
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            "TIMPRE=%lu): differing 0x%04lX", (unsigned long)hclk, (unsigned long)T_CLK_HZ,
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((RCC->D2CFGR >> 4) & 7U), (unsigned long)((RCC->D2CFGR >> 8) & 7U),
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((RCC->CFGR >> 15) & 1U), (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| + * T7: PWM on the unwired pins, IDR sampled against CYCCNT
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +typedef struct { uint32_t rises; uint32_t falls; uint32_t t_rise[16]; uint32_t t_fall[16]; uint32_t max_gap; uint32_t level; } Edges_t;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +/* Samples the pin for `cycles`: each sample reads CYCCNT and IDR with PRIMASK set (a pair
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +   the HAL tick cannot split); between samples interrupts run, and the largest gap is kept. */
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +static void sample_pin(const GPIO_TypeDef *port, uint32_t pin, uint32_t cycles, Edges_t *e)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    memset(e, 0, sizeof(*e));
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t prev_t = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t prev = (port->IDR >> pin) & 1UL;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    e->level = prev;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = prev_t;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    while ((prev_t - t0) < cycles)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        __disable_irq();
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t now = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t v = (port->IDR >> pin) & 1UL;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        if ((now - prev_t) > e->max_gap)
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            e->max_gap = now - prev_t;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        if ((v != 0U) && (prev == 0U) && (e->rises < 16U))
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +            e->t_rise[e->rises++] = now;
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.699s] [host] diff| +        else if ((v == 0U) && (prev != 0U) && (e->falls < 16U))
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            e->t_fall[e->falls++] = now;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        prev = v;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        prev_t = now;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +static void t7_one(TIM_TypeDef *t, GPIO_TypeDef *port, uint32_t pin, uint32_t af, const char *pname)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    Edges_t e;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    if (!pin_cfg(port, pin, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, af, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U))
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(t, 199U, 999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);   /* 200 MHz / 200 / 1000 = 1 kHz */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { t, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 250U, false, true };   /* 25 %                         */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const bool cfg_ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +                        (TIM_BM_OutputEnable(t, TIM_BM_CH(1)) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_Start(t) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    sample_pin(port, pin, 12U * CYC_PER_MS, &e);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    /* period: mean of the rise-to-rise intervals; high: mean of rise-to-next-fall */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t per_ok = 0U;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    int64_t per_sum = 0;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    int64_t hi_sum = 0;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 1U; k < e.rises; k++)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        per_sum += (int64_t)(uint32_t)(e.t_rise[k] - e.t_rise[k - 1U]);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        per_ok++;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t hi_n = 0U;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < e.rises; k++)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t f = 0U; f < e.falls; f++)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t d = e.t_fall[f] - e.t_rise[k];
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            if ((d > 0U) && (d < 400000U))
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +                hi_sum += d;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +                hi_n++;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +                break;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const int64_t exp_per = 1000LL * 200LL * CYC_PER_TICK;         /* 200000 ticks = 400000 cycles */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const int32_t per_ppm = (per_ok != 0U) ? (int32_t)(((per_sum / per_ok) - exp_per) * 1000000LL / exp_per) : 0;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const int32_t duty_x100 = ((per_ok != 0U) && (hi_n != 0U)) ? (int32_t)((hi_sum / hi_n) * 10000LL / (per_sum / per_ok)) : 0;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const bool ok = cfg_ok && (per_ok >= 10U) && (per_ppm >= -5000) && (per_ppm <= 5000) && (duty_x100 >= 2450) &&
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +                    (duty_x100 <= 2550);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(t);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_OutputDisable(t, TIM_BM_CH(1));
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    /* control: CCxE = 0, the pin follows its pull-down */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    Edges_t z;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Start(t);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    sample_pin(port, pin, 12U * CYC_PER_MS, &z);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(t);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const bool ctrl = (z.rises == 0U) && (z.falls == 0U) && (z.level == 0U);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ok && ctrl, "T7 %s on %s (1 kHz, 25 %%): %lu periods, period %+ld ppm (|e| <= 5000), duty %ld.%02ld %% "
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            "(25 +-0.5), largest sampling gap %lu cycles; control CC1E=0: %lu edges, level %lu", tim_name(t), pname,
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)per_ok, (long)per_ppm, (long)(duty_x100 / 100), (long)(duty_x100 % 100),
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)e.max_gap, (unsigned long)(z.rises + z.falls), (unsigned long)z.level);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)pin_cfg(port, pin, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(t, "T7");
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +static void test_t7(void)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    t7_one(TIM13, GPIOF, 8U, 9U, "PF8 AF9");
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    t7_one(TIM14, GPIOF, 9U, 9U, "PF9 AF9");
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    t7_one(TIM16, GPIOF, 6U, 1U, "PF6 AF1");
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    t7_one(TIM17, GPIOF, 7U, 1U, "PF7 AF1");
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t p = 6U; p <= 9U; p++)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        (void)pin_cfg(GPIOF, p, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| + * T10: master / slave, the slave's CNT and CCR read raw
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +/* Counts `n` updates of `t` by polling UIF (the period must leave the loop time), then
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +   stops it. Bounded by `max_cycles`. */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t run_updates(TIM_TypeDef *t, uint32_t n, uint32_t max_cycles)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t k = 0U;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    while ((k < n) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < max_cycles))
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(t, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +            k++;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(t);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    return k;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +static void t10a(bool control)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    /* TIM3 (MMS = update, period 2000 ticks) -> TIM2 external clock 1 on ITR2 (table 358).
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +       Control: ITR1 = TIM8, which is stopped. */
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t s = tb_cfg(TIM2, 0U, 0xFFFFFFFFUL, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t m = tb_cfg(TIM3, 0U, 1999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&s) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&m) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM3, TIM_BM_MMS_UPDATE, false) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM2, TIM_BM_SMS_EXT_CLOCK1, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(control ? 1U : 2U)) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_Start(TIM2) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_Start(TIM3) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t k = run_updates(TIM3, 1000U, 100U * CYC_PER_MS);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cnt = TIM2->CNT;
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM2);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    if (!control)
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (k == 1000U) && (cnt == 1000U), "T10(a) TIM3 update -> TIM2 external clock 1 (ITR2): %lu TIM3 updates, "
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +                "TIM2 CNT = %lu (1000 exactly)", (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)cnt);
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.700s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (k == 1000U) && (cnt == 0U), "T10(a) control: TIM2 on ITR1 (TIM8, stopped): TIM2 CNT = %lu (0)",
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)cnt);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM2, "T10(a)");
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM3, "T10(a)");
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +/* Gated: the master's OC1REF (PWM1, CCR high ticks per period) gates `slave`. OC1REF is
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +   first set by the master's first update (it stays low before it: the first dev run gave
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +   CNT = (N - 1) CCR), so the N periods are counted from that update, and the master stops
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +   in the low part of the period after the N-th, with the gate closed. */
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +static void t10_gated(TIM_TypeDef *master, uint32_t mms, TIM_TypeDef *slave, uint32_t itr, uint32_t n, uint32_t ccr,
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                      const char *what, bool control)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t ms = tb_cfg(master, 0U, 999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t sl = tb_cfg(slave, 0U, 0xFFFFU, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { master, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, ccr, false, true };
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&ms) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&sl) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(slave, TIM_BM_SMS_GATED, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(itr)) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    if (mms != 0xFFU)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok && (TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(master, mms, false) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        /* TIM13/TIM14 have no TRGO: table 361 wires their OC1 to ITR2/ITR3. CC1E on, the
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +           pin (PF8/PF9) is not in AF, so nothing is driven. */
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok && (TIM_BM_OutputEnable(master, TIM_BM_CH(1)) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    ok = ok && (TIM_BM_Start(slave) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_Start(master) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t k = 0U;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    while ((k < n) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (50U * CYC_PER_MS)))
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(master, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +            k++;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    while (((master->CNT) < (ccr + 20U)) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (60U * CYC_PER_MS)))
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(master);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cnt = slave->CNT;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(slave);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const int32_t diff = (int32_t)cnt - (int32_t)(n * ccr);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    if (!control)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (k == n) && (diff >= -(int32_t)n) && (diff <= (int32_t)n),
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                "T10%s: %lu periods of %lu high ticks, slave CNT = %lu (N CCR = %lu, |diff| <= N)", what, (unsigned long)n,
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)ccr, (unsigned long)cnt, (unsigned long)(n * ccr));
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (cnt == 0U), "T10%s control: the slave on an ITR whose master is stopped: CNT = %lu (0)", what,
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)cnt);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(master, "T10");
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(slave, "T10");
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +static void t10c(bool control)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    /* TIM1 TRGO (update) -> TIM8 trigger mode on ITR0 (table 353): TIM8 starts by itself.
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +       Control: ITR3 = TIM5, stopped. */
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t m = tb_cfg(TIM1, 0U, 1999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t s = tb_cfg(TIM8, 0U, 0xFFFFU, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&m) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM1, TIM_BM_MMS_UPDATE, false) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&s) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM8, TIM_BM_SMS_TRIGGER, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(control ? 3U : 0U)) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cen_before = TIM8->CR1 & TIM_CR1_CEN;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM1);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t k = run_updates(TIM1, 1U, CYC_PER_MS);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cen_after = TIM8->CR1 & TIM_CR1_CEN;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM8);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    if (!control)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (k == 1U) && (cen_before == 0U) && (cen_after != 0U),
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                "T10(c) TIM1 TRGO -> TIM8 trigger (ITR0): TIM8 CEN %lu before, %lu after the first TIM1 update",
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)cen_before, (unsigned long)(cen_after != 0U));
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (cen_after == 0U), "T10(c) control: TIM8 on ITR3 (TIM5, stopped): CEN %lu after (0)",
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)(cen_after != 0U));
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM1, "T10(c)");
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM8, "T10(c)");
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +/* (d) TRC capture of a master's TRGO: two captures, difference = master period. */
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +static void t10d_one(TIM_TypeDef *slave, uint32_t itr, TIM_TypeDef *master, bool control)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t period = 1000U;
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t m = tb_cfg(master, 0U, period - 1U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t s = tb_cfg(slave, 0U, (slave == TIM2 || slave == TIM5) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0xFFFFU,
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +                                      TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_IcConfig_t ic = { slave, 1U, TIM_BM_IC_TRC, TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, 1U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&s) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(slave, TIM_BM_SMS_DISABLED, TIM_BM_TRG_ITR(itr)) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_CaptureEnable(slave, TIM_BM_CH(1)) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +              (TIM_BM_Start(slave) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    if (!control)
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok && (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&m) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +             (TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(master, TIM_BM_MMS_UPDATE, false) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_Start(master) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.701s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t cap[3] = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    while ((n < 3U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < CYC_PER_MS))
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(slave, TIM_BM_EV_CC1) != 0U)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +            cap[n++] = TIM_BM_ReadCapture(slave, 1U);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(master);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(slave);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mask = (slave == TIM2 || slave == TIM5) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0xFFFFUL;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d = (cap[2] - cap[1]) & mask;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    if (!control)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (n == 3U) && (d >= (period - 1U)) && (d <= (period + 1U)),
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                "T10(d) %s captures TRC = ITR%lu (%s TRGO, period %lu ticks): difference %lu (+-1)", tim_name(slave),
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)itr, tim_name(master), (unsigned long)period, (unsigned long)d);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (n == 0U), "T10(d) control: %s on ITR%lu with %s stopped: %lu captures (0)", tim_name(slave),
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)itr, tim_name(master), (unsigned long)n);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(slave, "T10(d)");
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(master, "T10(d)");
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +static void test_t10(void)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10a(false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10a(true);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    /* (b) TIM2 (MMS = OC1REF) -> TIM4 gated on ITR1 (table 358): CNT = N CCR. Control: ITR3 = TIM8, stopped. */
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10_gated(TIM2, TIM_BM_MMS_OC1REF, TIM4, 1U, 100U, 200U, "(b) TIM2 OC1REF -> TIM4 gated (ITR1)", false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10_gated(TIM2, TIM_BM_MMS_OC1REF, TIM4, 3U, 100U, 200U, "(b)", true);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10c(false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10c(true);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    /* (d) every TRC slave of CPU1 with TIM1 as master (ITR0), TIM12 with TIM4 (ITR0). TIM15
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +       is CPU2's: T10D_ARM/READ. */
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM2, 0U, TIM1, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM3, 0U, TIM1, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM4, 0U, TIM1, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM5, 0U, TIM1, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM8, 0U, TIM1, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM12, 0U, TIM4, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10d_one(TIM2, 0U, TIM1, true);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        TstArea_t *a = TST_AREA;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t m = tb_cfg(TIM1, 0U, 999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        const bool arm = cm4_send(TST_CMD_T10D_ARM, 0U) && (a->m4_res[0] == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        const bool mst = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&m) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                         (TIM_BM_ConfigMaster(TIM1, TIM_BM_MMS_UPDATE, false) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                         (TIM_BM_Start(TIM1) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        const bool rd = cm4_send(TST_CMD_T10D_READ, 0U);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t d = (a->m4_res[4] - a->m4_res[3]) & 0xFFFFU;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        verdict(arm && mst && rd && (a->m4_res[1] >= 3U) && (d >= 999U) && (d <= 1001U) && (a->m4_res[6] == 1U),
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                "T10(d) TIM15 (CM4) captures TRC = ITR0 (TIM1 TRGO, period 1000 ticks): %lu captures, difference %lu "
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +                "(+-1), CM4 T14 %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[1], (unsigned long)d, (unsigned long)a->m4_res[6]);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        (void)t14(TIM1, "T10(d) TIM15");
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    /* (e) TIM13 / TIM14 OC1REF -> TIM12 gated on ITR2 / ITR3 (table 361). */
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10_gated(TIM13, 0xFFU, TIM12, 2U, 100U, 200U, "(e) TIM13 OC1 -> TIM12 gated (ITR2)", false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    t10_gated(TIM14, 0xFFU, TIM12, 3U, 100U, 200U, "(e) TIM14 OC1 -> TIM12 gated (ITR3)", false);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| + * T12: interrupts. The test ISRs: TakeFlags of their own timer, a counter each.
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +enum { IQ_T1UP, IQ_T1CC, IQ_T2, IQ_T3, IQ_T4, IQ_T5, IQ_T7, IQ_T8UP, IQ_T13, IQ_T12, IQ_T14, IQ_T8CC, IQ_T16, IQ_T17,
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +       IQ_SPUR, IQ_N };
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_irq[IQ_N];
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_on;    /* TIM7's vector serves T15 instead of T12 */
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +static void t15_isr(void);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +static void count_irq(TIM_TypeDef *t, uint32_t ev, uint32_t idx)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(t, ev) != 0U)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        s_irq[idx]++;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        s_irq[IQ_SPUR]++;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM1_UP_IRQHandler(void)  { count_irq(TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T1UP); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM1_CC_IRQHandler(void)  { count_irq(TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_CC1, IQ_T1CC); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM2_IRQHandler(void)     { count_irq(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T2); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM3_IRQHandler(void)     { count_irq(TIM3, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T3); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM4_IRQHandler(void)     { count_irq(TIM4, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T4); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM5_IRQHandler(void)     { count_irq(TIM5, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T5); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM8_CC_IRQHandler(void)  { count_irq(TIM8, TIM_BM_EV_CC1, IQ_T8CC); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM16_IRQHandler(void)    { count_irq(TIM16, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T16); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM17_IRQHandler(void)    { count_irq(TIM17, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T17); }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM7_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    if (s_t15_on != 0U)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        t15_isr();
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +        count_irq(TIM7, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, IQ_T7);
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +/* Shared vectors: the ISR tells the instances apart with TakeFlags. */
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +void TIM8_UP_TIM13_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.702s] [host] diff| +    if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM8, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U)  { s_irq[IQ_T8UP]++; n++; }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM13, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U) { s_irq[IQ_T13]++; n++; }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    if (n == 0U) { s_irq[IQ_SPUR]++; }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +void TIM8_BRK_TIM12_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM12, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U) { s_irq[IQ_T12]++; } else { s_irq[IQ_SPUR]++; }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +void TIM8_TRG_COM_TIM14_IRQHandler(void)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    if (TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM14, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) != 0U) { s_irq[IQ_T14]++; } else { s_irq[IQ_SPUR]++; }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +/* The vectors, by hand from stm32h755xx.h (the T12(c) oracle). */
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +typedef struct { TIM_TypeDef *tim; uint32_t ev; IRQn_Type irq; uint32_t idx; } IrqRow_t;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +static const IrqRow_t k_irq_rows[] = {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM1_UP_IRQn, IQ_T1UP },          { TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_CC1, TIM1_CC_IRQn, IQ_T1CC },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM2_IRQn, IQ_T2 },               { TIM3, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM3_IRQn, IQ_T3 },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM4, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM4_IRQn, IQ_T4 },               { TIM5, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM5_IRQn, IQ_T5 },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM7, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM7_IRQn, IQ_T7 },               { TIM8, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM8_UP_TIM13_IRQn, IQ_T8UP },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM13, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM8_UP_TIM13_IRQn, IQ_T13 },    { TIM12, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM8_BRK_TIM12_IRQn, IQ_T12 },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM14, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM8_TRG_COM_TIM14_IRQn, IQ_T14 }, { TIM8, TIM_BM_EV_CC1, TIM8_CC_IRQn, IQ_T8CC },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    { TIM16, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM16_IRQn, IQ_T16 },            { TIM17, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, TIM17_IRQn, IQ_T17 },
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +};
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +#define N_IRQ_ROWS   (sizeof(k_irq_rows) / sizeof(k_irq_rows[0]))
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +static void irq_on(IRQn_Type irq)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_SetPriority(irq, TEST_IRQ_PRIO);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(irq);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_EnableIRQ(irq);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +static void irq_off(IRQn_Type irq)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_DisableIRQ(irq);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(irq);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +/* 10 kHz: PSC + 1 = 200, ARR + 1 = 100. A CC row uses CC1 in frozen mode at CCR1 = 50. */
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +static bool t12_arm(const IrqRow_t *r)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(r->tim, 199U, 99U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    if (r->ev == TIM_BM_EV_CC1)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { r->tim, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_FROZEN, 50U, false, false };
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok && (TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    return ok && (TIM_BM_EnableIrq(r->tim, r->ev) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +/* Runs the rows of `set` (bit k = row k) together for 100 ms. */
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +static void t12_group(uint32_t set, const char *what)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = true;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < IQ_N; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        s_irq[k] = 0U;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < N_IRQ_ROWS; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if ((set & (1UL << k)) != 0U)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            ok = ok && t12_arm(&k_irq_rows[k]);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            irq_on(k_irq_rows[k].irq);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < N_IRQ_ROWS; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if ((set & (1UL << k)) != 0U)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_Start(k_irq_rows[k].tim);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    delay_cycles(100U * CYC_PER_MS);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < N_IRQ_ROWS; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if ((set & (1UL << k)) != 0U)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_Stop(k_irq_rows[k].tim);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < N_IRQ_ROWS; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if ((set & (1UL << k)) != 0U)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            irq_off(k_irq_rows[k].irq);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_DisableIrq(k_irq_rows[k].tim, k_irq_rows[k].ev);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    /* each counter of the set at 1000 +-1, every other counter at 0 */
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < N_IRQ_ROWS; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t n = s_irq[k_irq_rows[k].idx];
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        const bool in = (set & (1UL << k)) != 0U;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if (in ? ((n < 999U) || (n > 1001U)) : (n != 0U))
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            ok = false;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    char buf[96];
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    int len = 0;
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; (k < N_IRQ_ROWS) && (len < 80); k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if ((set & (1UL << k)) != 0U)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            len += snprintf(&buf[len], sizeof(buf) - (size_t)len, " %s=%lu", tim_name(k_irq_rows[k].tim),
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +                            (unsigned long)s_irq[k_irq_rows[k].idx]);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ok && (s_irq[IQ_SPUR] == 0U), "T12(a) %s, 10 kHz for 100 ms:%s (1000 +-1), others 0, ISR calls that took "
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            "nothing %lu", what, buf, (unsigned long)s_irq[IQ_SPUR]);
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < N_IRQ_ROWS; k++)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        if ((set & (1UL << k)) != 0U)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +            (void)t14(k_irq_rows[k].tim, "T12");
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +static void test_t12(void)
[+   1.703s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    typedef struct { TIM_TypeDef *t; int16_t up, cc, trg; } IrqHand_t;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    static const IrqHand_t k_hand[] = {
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +        { TIM1, TIM1_UP_IRQn, TIM1_CC_IRQn, TIM1_TRG_COM_IRQn },   { TIM2, TIM2_IRQn, TIM2_IRQn, TIM2_IRQn },
