[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            const bool ok = (exp[b] == 0U) ? (st == TIM_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED) : ((st == TIM_BM_OK) && (id == exp[b]));
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            checked++;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            if (!ok)
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +                bad++;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +                logw("T13(c) %s event 0x%02lX: status %u id %lu, DMA_REQUEST_* %lu", tim_name(k_hand[i].t),
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +                    (unsigned long)(1UL << b), (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)id, (unsigned long)exp[b]);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T13(c) GetDmaRequest against DMA_REQUEST_TIM* (stm32h7xx_hal_dma.h): %lu pairs, %lu differ",
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)checked, (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| + * T14 control: a timer left configured on purpose must be flagged
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static void test_t14_control(void)
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(TIM2, 5U, 1234U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t idx = 0U;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t got = 0U;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t exp = 0U;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    const bool flagged = !tst_reset_ok(TIM2, &idx, &got, &exp);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    const bool after = t14(TIM2, "T14 control");
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    verdict(flagged && after, "T14 control: TIM2 left with PSC = 5, ARR = 1234 is flagged (%s reads 0x%08lX, reset "
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            "0x%08lX); after Deinit the check passes %u", k_ri_name[idx], (unsigned long)got, (unsigned long)exp,
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)after);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| + * T15: concurrency on one core (C10). The thread toggles CC2IE of TIM2 while a TIM7 ISR at
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| + * 50 kHz toggles CC3IE. A lost update: the ISR finds CC3IE other than it last wrote.
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_driver;   /* 1: both sides through tim_bm; 0: the control's hand RMW */
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_in_rmw;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_isr;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_lost;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_overlap;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static volatile uint32_t s_t15_cc3;      /* CC3IE as the ISR last wrote it */
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +static void t15_isr(void)
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM7, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE);
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_isr++;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    if (s_t15_in_rmw != 0U)
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +        s_t15_overlap++;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t now = ((TIM2->DIER & TIM_DIER_CC3IE) != 0U) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    if (now != s_t15_cc3)
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +        s_t15_lost++;
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    if (s_t15_driver != 0U)
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +        (void)((s_t15_cc3 != 0U) ? TIM_BM_DisableIrq(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_CC3) : TIM_BM_EnableIrq(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_CC3));
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.704s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        TIM2->DIER = (s_t15_cc3 != 0U) ? (TIM2->DIER & ~TIM_DIER_CC3IE) : (TIM2->DIER | TIM_DIER_CC3IE);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_cc3 = (s_t15_cc3 != 0U) ? 0U : 1U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +#define T15_ITER        1000000UL
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +#define T15_RMW_WINDOW  24U        /* cycles between the read and the write in the control */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static void t15_run(bool driver)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_driver = driver ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_isr = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_lost = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_overlap = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_cc3 = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TIM2->DIER = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(TIM7, 0U, 3999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);   /* 200 MHz / 4000 = 50 kHz */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_EnableIrq(TIM7, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_on = 1U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    irq_on(TIM7_IRQn);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM7);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < T15_ITER; i++)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        if (driver)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_EnableIrq(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_CC2);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_DisableIrq(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_CC2);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t d = TIM2->DIER;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            s_t15_in_rmw = 1U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            delay_cycles(T15_RMW_WINDOW);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            TIM2->DIER = d | TIM_DIER_CC2IE;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            s_t15_in_rmw = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            d = TIM2->DIER;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            s_t15_in_rmw = 1U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            delay_cycles(T15_RMW_WINDOW);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            TIM2->DIER = d & ~TIM_DIER_CC2IE;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            s_t15_in_rmw = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM7);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    irq_off(TIM7_IRQn);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    s_t15_on = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    /* the last ISR's write must still be there (one more check of the final state) */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t final_cc3 = ((TIM2->DIER & TIM_DIER_CC3IE) != 0U) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    if (final_cc3 != s_t15_cc3)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        s_t15_lost++;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t final_dier = TIM2->DIER;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    if (driver)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        verdict((s_t15_lost == 0U) && (s_t15_isr > 1000U) && ((final_dier & TIM_DIER_CC2IE) == 0U),
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                "T15 tim_bm: %lu x Enable/DisableIrq(TIM2, CC2) against %lu TIM7 ISRs toggling CC3: %lu lost updates (0), "
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                "final DIER 0x%04lX", (unsigned long)T15_ITER, (unsigned long)s_t15_isr, (unsigned long)s_t15_lost,
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)final_dier);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        verdict((s_t15_lost > 0U) && (s_t15_overlap > 0U),
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                "T15 control (RMW by hand, no PRIMASK, %u-cycle window): %lu ISRs, %lu inside the thread's RMW window, "
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                "%lu lost updates (> 0)", (unsigned)T15_RMW_WINDOW, (unsigned long)s_t15_isr,
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)s_t15_overlap, (unsigned long)s_t15_lost);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TIM2->DIER = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM7, "T15");
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM2, "T15");
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static void test_t15(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    t15_run(true);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    t15_run(false);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| + * T16: the RCC banks of the two cores (RM0399 9.5.10)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static bool tim2_counting(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t a = TIM2->CNT;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    delay_cycles(2000U);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    return TIM2->CNT != a;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static void test_t16(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TstArea_t *a = TST_AREA;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = tb_cfg(TIM2, 0U, 0xFFFFFFFFUL, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, false);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool on = (TIM_BM_EnableClock(TIM2) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                    (TIM_BM_Start(TIM2) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool run0 = tim2_counting();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool s1 = cm4_send(TST_CMD_T16, 0U) && (a->m4_res[0] == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c1_1 = RCC_C1->APB1LENR & RCC_APB1LENR_TIM2EN;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c2_1 = RCC_C2->APB1LENR & RCC_APB1LENR_TIM2EN;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool run1 = tim2_counting();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool s2 = cm4_send(TST_CMD_T16, 1U) && (a->m4_res[0] == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c2_mid = a->m4_res[3] & RCC_APB1LENR_TIM2EN;   /* CPU2's bank after its EnableClock */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c2_2 = RCC_C2->APB1LENR & RCC_APB1LENR_TIM2EN;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool run2 = tim2_counting();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_DisableClock(TIM2);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c1_3 = RCC_C1->APB1LENR & RCC_APB1LENR_TIM2EN;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const bool run3 = tim2_counting();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    verdict(on && run0 && s1 && (c1_1 != 0U) && (c2_1 == 0U) && run1,
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            "T16 CM4 DisableClock(TIM2) while CM7 enables it: RCC_C1 bit %lu, RCC_C2 bit %lu, TIM2 still counting %u",
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(c1_1 != 0U), (unsigned long)(c2_1 != 0U), (unsigned)run1);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    verdict(s2 && (c2_mid != 0U) && (c2_2 == 0U) && run2,
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            "T16 CM4 EnableClock then DisableClock(TIM2): RCC_C2 bit %lu then %lu, TIM2 still counting %u",
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(c2_mid != 0U), (unsigned long)(c2_2 != 0U), (unsigned)run2);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    verdict((c1_3 == 0U) && !run3, "T16 control: CM7 DisableClock(TIM2) with RCC_C2 clear: RCC_C1 bit %lu, TIM2 counting %u (0)",
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(c1_3 != 0U), (unsigned)run3);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_EnableClock(TIM2);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM2, "T16");
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| + * B1: cycles of the plan's functions on CPU1 (CPU2: TST_CMD_B1)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t cycles_of(TimBm_Status_t (*fn)(void))
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    __disable_irq();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)fn();
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c = DWT->CYCCNT - c0;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    return c;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static TimBm_Status_t b1_take(void)  { (void)TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM2, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE); return TIM_BM_OK; }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static TimBm_Status_t b1_oe(void)    { return TIM_BM_OutputEnable(TIM2, TIM_BM_CH(1)); }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static TimBm_Status_t b1_oc(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { TIM2, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    return TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static TimBm_Status_t b1_tb(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM2, 0U, 999U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, true, false, false };
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    return TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static void test_b1(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TstArea_t *a = TST_AREA;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t tb = cycles_of(b1_tb);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t oc = cycles_of(b1_oc);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t oe = cycles_of(b1_oe);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TIM2->SR = 0U;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t tk = cycles_of(b1_take);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM2, "B1");
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    logw("B1 CM7 cycles (400 MHz): TakeFlags %lu, OutputEnable %lu, ConfigOutput %lu, InitTimeBase %lu",
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)tk, (unsigned long)oe, (unsigned long)oc, (unsigned long)tb);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(TST_CMD_B1, 0U))
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        logw("B1 CM4 cycles (200 MHz, TIM15): TakeFlags %lu, OutputEnable %lu, ConfigOutput %lu, InitTimeBase %lu",
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)a->m4_res[0], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[1], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[2],
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)a->m4_res[3]);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| + * CPU2's F1 tests (TIM15), judged here
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +static void test_cm4(void)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    TstArea_t *a = TST_AREA;
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(TST_CMD_T1, 0U))
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        verdict((a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_BAD] == 0U) && (a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_TOUCHED] == 0U) && (a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CASES] > 0U),
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                "T1 CM4 TIM15: %lu refusals, wrong status 0x%04lX, snapshot changed 0x%04lX", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CASES],
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_BAD], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_TOUCHED]);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CASES]; k++)
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T1 CM4 case %lu: status %lu", (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_STATUS0 + k]);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +        verdict((a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CTRL_ST] == TIM_BM_OK) && (a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CTRL_DIFF] == a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CTRL_EXP]),
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                "T1 CM4 control (O1): ConfigOutput(TIM15 CH1) changes TIM15 words 0x%07lX (0x%07lX: CCMR1, CCR1), status %lu",
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CTRL_DIFF], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CTRL_EXP],
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T1_CTRL_ST]);
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(TST_CMD_T2, 0U))
[+   1.705s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        verdict((a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_MISMATCH] == 0U) && (a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_CALLS_BAD] == 0U) &&
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_CTRL] == 1U) && (a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_CONFIGS] > 0U),
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                "T2 CM4 TIM15: %lu images, mismatches %lu (first: image %lu word 0x%02lX reads 0x%08lX, hand 0x%08lX), "
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                "calls not OK %lu, altered image flagged %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_CONFIGS],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_MISMATCH], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_FIRST_CFG],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)(a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_FIRST_REG] * 4U), (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_FIRST_GOT],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_FIRST_EXP], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_CALLS_BAD],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[TST_R_T2_CTRL]);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(TST_CMD_T5, 0U))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        /* CPU2 at 200 MHz: 1 cycle per tick */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < 2U; m++)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t ticks = a->m4_res[3U * m];
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t n = a->m4_res[3U * m + 1U];
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t span = a->m4_res[3U * m + 2U];
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const int64_t exp = (int64_t)(n - 1U) * ticks;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const int64_t expc = (int64_t)(n - 1U) * ((m == 0U) ? 1500U : 1000U);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const int32_t err = (n >= 3U) ? (int32_t)((((int64_t)span - exp) * 1000000) / exp) : 0;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            const int32_t ctrl = (n >= 3U) ? (int32_t)((((int64_t)span - expc) * 1000000) / expc) : 0;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            verdict((n >= 3U) && (err >= -100) && (err <= 100) && ((ctrl < -100) || (ctrl > 100)),
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                    "T5 TIM15 (CM4) %s: %lu updates over %lu cycles, %lu ticks each: %+ld ppm (|e| <= 100); control: %+ld ppm",
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                    (m == 0U) ? "(a) up, PSC=1 ARR=499" : "(b) RCR=3", (unsigned long)n, (unsigned long)span,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                    (unsigned long)ticks, (long)err, (long)ctrl);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        verdict((a->m4_res[7] == TIM_BM_OK) && (a->m4_res[6] == T_CLK_HZ),
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                "T5(e) CM4 GetKernelClockHz(TIM15, hclk %lu) = %lu Hz, status %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[9],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[6], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[7]);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        verdict(a->m4_res[8] == 1U, "T14 CM4 TIM15 after T5: %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[8]);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send(TST_CMD_T12, 0U))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        verdict((a->m4_res[0] >= 999U) && (a->m4_res[0] <= 1001U) && (a->m4_res[2] == 0U) && (a->m4_res[3] == 1U) &&
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (a->m4_res[4] == 0U) && (a->m4_res[6] == 1U),
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                "T12 TIM15 (CM4): (a) %lu update IRQs in %lu cycles (1000 +-1); (b) %lu spurious (0), control by hand %lu (1); "
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                "UIE = 0: %lu (0); GetIRQn = TIM15_IRQn %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[0], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[1],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[2], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[3], (unsigned long)a->m4_res[4],
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)a->m4_res[6]);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        verdict(a->m4_res[5] == 1U, "T14 CM4 TIM15 after T12: %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[5]);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +/* The internal F1 tests, between the T0b steps and the AD2 steps. No wired pin moves. */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static void f1_internal(void)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    f1_clocks_on();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t3();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t1();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t2();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t14_control();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t5();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t7();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t10();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t12();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t13c();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t15();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_t16();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_b1();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    test_cm4();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| + * The AD2 steps of F1. R3-2: MCO1 = HSE/8 on PA8 (AF0) in the T6 and T8 steps (and in T9),
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| + * so every frequency the AD2 measures is a ratio with MCO1 in the same acquisition; RCC_CFGR
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| + * is saved before and restored with read-back after. T11 and T13(a) run inside the T6 step
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| + * (TIM1 outputs, bus = MCU) and T13(b) inside the T8 step (TIM1 inputs, bus = AD2), so they
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| + * have MCO1 too and no extra step writes RCC_CFGR (instructions 3.5).
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_cfgr_step;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static bool mco1_on(const char *step)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    s_cfgr_step   = RCC->CFGR;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    s_mco_saved   = s_cfgr_step & (RCC_CFGR_MCO1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    s_mco_changed = true;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    MODIFY_REG(RCC->CFGR, RCC_CFGR_MCO1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE, RCC_CFGR_MCO1_1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE_3);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t on = RCC->CFGR & (RCC_CFGR_MCO1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (!pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_MCO, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_MCO1, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    delay_ms(2U);     /* the first ms after an MCO1 change may glitch (RM0399 9.5.4) */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    verdict(on == (RCC_CFGR_MCO1_1 | RCC_CFGR_MCO1PRE_3), "%s MCO1 = HSE/8 on PA8 AF0 (R3-2): MCO1 fields 0x%08lX", step,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)on);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static void mco1_off(const char *step)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    (void)pin_cfg(GPIOA, PA_MCO, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    mco_restore();
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t after = RCC->CFGR;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    verdict(after == s_cfgr_step, "%s RCC_CFGR restored: before 0x%08lX after 0x%08lX", step,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)s_cfgr_step, (unsigned long)after);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +/* One handshake with an action between HOST_REQ rising and MCU_ACK falling: the host has
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +   armed or started its instrument when it raises HOST_REQ. */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static bool sync_act(void (*fn)(void *), void *arg)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (s_bench_abort)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    ack(true);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = wait_req(true, SYNC_TIMEOUT_CYC);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (ok)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        fn(arg);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    ack(false);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (ok)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        ok = wait_req(false, SYNC_TIMEOUT_CYC);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("host timeout");
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +#define T6_TIM1_PINS   ((1UL << 9) | (1UL << 11) | (1UL << 13) | (1UL << 14))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +/* T11: 10 one-pulses by software, 3 us apart. CH1 = the pulse (PWM2, CCR1 = D), CH2 = a
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +   reference that rises one tick after the start (PWM2, CCR2 = 1); both end at the update. */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +#define T11_CCR1    40U
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +#define T11_ARR     139U
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +#define T11_PULSES  10U
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t11_fired;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static void t11_fire(void *arg)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    (void)arg;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    s_t11_fired = 0U;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < T11_PULSES; k++)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        while ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < (k * 1200U))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM1);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t s = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        while (((TIM1->CR1 & TIM_CR1_CEN) != 0U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - s) < 4000U))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        s_t11_fired++;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +/* T13(a): DMA1 stream 0, TIM1 UP (request 15) -> CCR1, 16 values, circular. */
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +#define T13_STREAM_A   DMA1_Stream0
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +#define T13_STREAM_B   DMA1_Stream1
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t13a_seq[16] __attribute__((section(".dma_buf"), aligned(32)));
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t13b_buf[64] __attribute__((section(".dma_buf"), aligned(32)));
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static bool t13_dma(DMA_Stream_TypeDef *s, uint32_t req, uint32_t dir, uint32_t periph, uint32_t mem, uint32_t n, bool circ)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    const DmaBm_Config_t c = {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        .stream = s, .request_id = req, .direction = dir,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        .periph_data_size = DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_WORD, .mem_data_size = DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_WORD,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        .periph_inc = false, .mem_inc = true, .circular = circ, .priority = DMA_BM_PRIORITY_HIGH,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        .enable_it_ht = false, .enable_it_tc = false, .enable_it_te = false, .enable_it_dme = false,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        .enable_it_fe = false, .enable_trbuff = false, .enable_fifo = false, .fifo_threshold = DMA_BM_FIFO_TH_1_2,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        .periph_addr = periph, .mem_addr = mem, .buffer_length = n,
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    };
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (!DMA_BM_InitStream(&c))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    DMA_BM_ClearFlags(s, DMA_BM_FLAG_ALL);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    DMA_BM_Start(s);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +static void step_t6_tim1(void)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("T6_TIM1") || !buschk() || !mco1_on("T6_TIM1"))
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM1);
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t p = 9U; p <= 14U; p++)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        if (((T6_TIM1_PINS >> p) & 1U) != 0U)
[+   1.706s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            if (!pin_cfg(GPIOE, p, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_TIM1, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U))
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    /* T6: every point on every channel; the other three channels stay disabled. */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t ch = 1U; ch <= 4U; ch++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < TST_T6_POINTS; k++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            const TstT6Point_t *p = &k_tst_t6[k];
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            const TimBm_Status_t st = tst_t6_apply(TIM1, ch, p);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T6 TIM1 CH%lu P%lu psc=%u arr=%u mode=%u ccr=%u low=%u center=%u st=%u", (unsigned long)ch,
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)k, p->psc, p->arr, p->mode, p->ccr, p->low, p->center, (unsigned)st);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            if (st != TIM_BM_OK)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                s_fail++;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            if (!sync0())                       /* host: LA on DIO1/3/5/6 and DIO14 */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    /* control: all four channels configured and enabled, MOE cleared (OSSI = 0) */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM1, 0U, 199U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, true, false, false };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t ch = 1U; ch <= 4U; ch++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            const TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { TIM1, ch, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_OutputEnable(TIM1, 0xFU);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        TIM1->BDTR &= ~TIM_BDTR_MOE;           /* the control, by hand */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        logw("T6 TIM1 control: CH1-4 PWM1 50 %% enabled, MOE = 0 (BDTR 0x%08lX), OSSI = 0", (unsigned long)TIM1->BDTR);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync0())
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    /* T11 */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM1, 0U, T11_ARR, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, false, true, false };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_OcConfig_t o1 = { TIM1, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM2, T11_CCR1, false, false };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_OcConfig_t o2 = { TIM1, 2U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM2, 1U, false, false };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const bool ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&o1) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                        (TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&o2) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_OutputEnable(TIM1, 0x3U) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        logw("T11 TIM1 one-pulse: CH1 PWM2 CCR1=%u, CH2 PWM2 CCR2=1 (reference), ARR=%u, %u pulses by software, config %u",
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)T11_CCR1, (unsigned)T11_ARR, (unsigned)T11_PULSES, (unsigned)ok);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync_act(t11_fire, NULL))         /* host: LA armed on DIO1 rising */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        verdict(ok && (s_t11_fired == T11_PULSES), "T11 %lu software triggers fired, CEN cleared by the hardware each time",
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)s_t11_fired);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        /* control: OPM = 0, periodic */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c2 = { TIM1, 0U, T11_ARR, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, false, false, false };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c2);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T11 control: OPM = 0, the same channels run periodically");
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync0())
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    /* T13(a) */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 16U; k++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            s_t13a_seq[k] = 10U * (k + 1U);    /* high times 10..160 ticks */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM1, 0U, 199U, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, true, false, false };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const TimBm_OcConfig_t oc = { TIM1, 1U, TIM_BM_OC_PWM1, 100U, false, true };
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t req = 0U;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        const bool ok = (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&oc) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                        (TIM_BM_GetDmaRequest(TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE, &req) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                        t13_dma(T13_STREAM_A, req, DMA_BM_DIR_MEM_TO_PERIPH, (uint32_t)&TIM1->CCR1,
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                                (uint32_t)s_t13a_seq, 16U, true) &&
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                        (TIM_BM_EnableDmaRequest(TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                        (TIM_BM_OutputEnable(TIM1, TIM_BM_CH(1)) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_Start(TIM1) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        logw("T13A TIM1 UP (request %lu) -> DMA1 stream 0 -> CCR1, 16 values 10..160 ticks, circular, PWM1 ARR=199: config %u",
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)req, (unsigned)ok);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (!ok)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            s_fail++;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync0())
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        /* control: UDE = 0, CCR1 stays */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_DisableDmaRequest(TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_UPDATE);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)DMA_BM_Stop(T13_STREAM_A);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        TIM_BM_WriteCcr(TIM1, 1U, 100U);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        delay_ms(1U);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T13A control: UDE = 0, CCR1 = 100");
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync0())
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (void)DMA_BM_DeinitStream(T13_STREAM_A);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t p = 9U; p <= 14U; p++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        if (((T6_TIM1_PINS >> p) & 1U) != 0U)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            (void)pin_cfg(GPIOE, p, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    (void)t14(TIM1, "T6/T11/T13(a)");
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    mco1_off("T6_TIM1");
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    const bool released = buschk();            /* T14: S5 on the wired pins */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    verdict(released, "T14 T6_TIM1: after Deinit the bus follows both pulls again");
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    step_end();
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +static void step_t6_tim15(void)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    TstArea_t *a = TST_AREA;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("T6_TIM15") || !mco1_on("T6_TIM15"))
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t ch = 1U; ch <= 2U; ch++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < (TST_T6_POINTS - 1U); k++)
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            const TstT6Point_t *p = &k_tst_t6[k];
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            const bool sent = cm4_send(TST_CMD_T6, (ch << 8) | k);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T6 TIM15 CH%lu P%lu psc=%u arr=%u mode=%u ccr=%u low=%u center=%u st=%lu", (unsigned long)ch,
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)k, p->psc, p->arr, p->mode, p->ccr, p->low, p->center,
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                sent ? (unsigned long)a->m4_res[0] : 99UL);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            if (!sent || (a->m4_res[0] != 0U))
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                s_fail++;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            if (!sync0())
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    (void)cm4_send(TST_CMD_T6, TST_T6_CONTROL);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    logw("T6 TIM15 control: CH1/CH2 configured, CC1E = CC2E = 0, pins follow the pull-down (status %lu)",
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)a->m4_res[0]);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    if (!sync0())
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    (void)cm4_send(TST_CMD_T6, TST_T6_OFF);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    verdict(a->m4_res[1] == 1U, "T14 CM4 TIM15 after T6: %lu", (unsigned long)a->m4_res[1]);
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    mco1_off("T6_TIM15");
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    step_end();
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T8 (+ T13(b)): captures of the AD2's Digital Out on PE9 (TI1) and PE11 (TI2) -------- */
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +typedef struct { uint8_t ti; uint32_t f; uint8_t duty; uint8_t psc; } T8Point_t;
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +static const T8Point_t k_t8[] = {
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 1U, 1000U, 25U, 3U }, { 1U, 1000U, 50U, 3U }, { 1U, 1000U, 75U, 3U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 1U, 10000U, 25U, 0U }, { 1U, 10000U, 50U, 0U }, { 1U, 10000U, 75U, 0U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 1U, 100000U, 25U, 0U }, { 1U, 100000U, 50U, 0U }, { 1U, 100000U, 75U, 0U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 1U, 1000000U, 25U, 0U }, { 1U, 1000000U, 50U, 0U }, { 1U, 1000000U, 75U, 0U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 2U, 1000U, 25U, 3U }, { 2U, 1000U, 50U, 3U }, { 2U, 1000U, 75U, 3U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 2U, 10000U, 25U, 0U }, { 2U, 10000U, 50U, 0U }, { 2U, 10000U, 75U, 0U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 2U, 100000U, 25U, 0U }, { 2U, 100000U, 50U, 0U }, { 2U, 100000U, 75U, 0U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +    { 2U, 1000000U, 25U, 0U }, { 2U, 1000000U, 50U, 0U }, { 2U, 1000000U, 75U, 0U },
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +};
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +#define T8_POINTS   (sizeof(k_t8) / sizeof(k_t8[0]))
[+   1.707s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +/* Reconfigure TIM1 without ever leaving CCxS = 00 on CH1/CH2 (S12 holds while the pins
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +   are in AF and the AD2 drives): captures off, counter stopped, slave mode off. */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static bool t8_quiesce(void)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    /* CaptureDisable refuses outputs (CCxS = 00): the first call after Deinit has no
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +       capture to disable, and CC1E = CC2E = 0 is what matters. */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_Status_t cd = TIM_BM_CaptureDisable(TIM1, 0x3U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const bool off = (cd == TIM_BM_OK) || ((TIM1->CCER & (TIM_CCER_CC1E | TIM_CCER_CC2E)) == 0U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    return off && (TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +           (TIM_BM_ConfigSlave(TIM1, TIM_BM_SMS_DISABLED, TIM_BM_TRG_TI1FP1) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static bool t8_pwm_input(uint32_t ti, uint32_t edge, uint32_t psc)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM1, psc, 0xFFFFU, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, false, false, false };
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    return t8_quiesce() && (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +           (TIM_BM_ConfigPwmInput(TIM1, ti, edge, 1U, 0U, 0U) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +/* PWM input: 8 captures after a discarded first one. Period on CCR(ti), high on the other.
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +   Every capture is printed: the host judges each one. */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +#define T8_CAPS   8U
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t8_n;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t8_per[T8_CAPS];
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t8_hi[T8_CAPS];
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static void t8_measure(uint32_t ti, uint32_t max_wait_cyc)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t ev = (ti == 1U) ? TIM_BM_EV_CC1 : TIM_BM_EV_CC2;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t other = (ti == 1U) ? 2U : 1U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    s_t8_n = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_CaptureEnable(TIM1, 0x3U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM1);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k <= T8_CAPS; k++)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t taken = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        if (TIM_BM_WaitFlags(TIM1, ev, max_wait_cyc, &taken) != TIM_BM_OK)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t per = TIM_BM_ReadCapture(TIM1, ti);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t hi = TIM_BM_ReadCapture(TIM1, other);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        if (k == 0U)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +            continue;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        s_t8_per[s_t8_n] = per;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        s_t8_hi[s_t8_n] = hi;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        s_t8_n++;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_CaptureDisable(TIM1, 0x3U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +/* "<tag> n=<n> per <8 values> hi <8 values>" (fits App_Log's 160 characters). */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static void t8_log_pwm(const char *tag)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    char per[64];
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    char hi[64];
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    int lp = 0;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    int lh = 0;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < s_t8_n; k++)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        lp += snprintf(&per[lp], sizeof(per) - (size_t)lp, " %lu", (unsigned long)s_t8_per[k]);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        lh += snprintf(&hi[lh], sizeof(hi) - (size_t)lh, " %lu", (unsigned long)s_t8_hi[k]);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    per[(lp > 0) ? lp : 0] = '\0';
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    hi[(lh > 0) ? lh : 0] = '\0';
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    LOG("%s n=%lu per%s hi%s", tag, (unsigned long)s_t8_n, per, hi);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +/* Direct capture on CH1 (CH2 stays an input): `n` successive CCR1 differences. */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t8_diff[16];
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t t8_diffs(uint32_t n, uint32_t max_wait_cyc)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t got = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t prev = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_CaptureEnable(TIM1, TIM_BM_CH(1));
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Start(TIM1);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k <= n; k++)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t taken = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        if (TIM_BM_WaitFlags(TIM1, TIM_BM_EV_CC1, max_wait_cyc, &taken) != TIM_BM_OK)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t v = TIM_BM_ReadCapture(TIM1, 1U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        if (k != 0U)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +            s_t8_diff[got++] = (v - prev) & 0xFFFFU;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        prev = v;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_CaptureDisable(TIM1, TIM_BM_CH(1));
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_Stop(TIM1);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    return got;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static bool t8_direct(uint32_t prescaler, uint32_t filter)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t c = { TIM1, 0U, 0xFFFFU, TIM_BM_COUNT_UP, 0U, false, false, false };
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    const TimBm_IcConfig_t ic = { TIM1, 1U, TIM_BM_IC_DIRECT, TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, prescaler, filter, 0U };
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    return t8_quiesce() && (TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&c) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic) == TIM_BM_OK);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static void t8_log_diffs(const char *what, uint32_t n)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    char buf[100];
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    int len = 0;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; (k < n) && (len < 88); k++)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        len += snprintf(&buf[len], sizeof(buf) - (size_t)len, " %lu", (unsigned long)s_t8_diff[k]);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    LOG("%s n=%lu diffs%s", what, (unsigned long)n, buf);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +static void step_t8(void)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("T8") || !buschk() || !mco1_on("T8"))
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    /* S12: TIM1 CH1/CH2 inputs before the pins go to AF1 */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    if (!t8_pwm_input(1U, TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, 0U))
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("T8 TIM1 input config");
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    if (!pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_CAP1, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_TIM1, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U) ||
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        !pin_cfg(GPIOE, PE_CAP2, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_TIM1, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 0U))
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t up = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t dn = 0U;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    if (!buschk_af((1UL << PE_CAP1) | (1UL << PE_CAP2), &up, &dn))   /* S5 with the pins in AF */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t p = PE_CAP1; p <= PE_CAP2; p += 2U)
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        (void)pin_cfg(GPIOE, p, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, TST_AF_TIM1, GPIO_BM_PULL_DOWN, 0U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    /* The counter-reset delay of the slave reset mode (RM0399 40.4.3: "due to the
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +       resynchronization circuit on TI1 input", no value given), measured in this session
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +       on one 1 MHz Digital Out generation: PWM input first, then direct captures of the
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +       same signal. The host subtracts it from the expected period and high time (owner's
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +       decision of 2026-10-02, after the first F1 development runs). */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        const bool ok = t8_pwm_input(1U, TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, 0U);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T8 DELAY ti=1 f=1000000 duty=50 config %u", (unsigned)ok);
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        if (!sync0())                          /* host: Digital Out 1 MHz on DIO1, LA */
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.708s] [host] diff| +            return;

[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_t9_n;

[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +static void t9_capture(void *arg)
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    (void)arg;
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    s_t9_n = 0U;
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    s_t9_min = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;

[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t prev = 0U;
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    (void)TIM_BM_TakeFlags(TIM17, TIM_BM_EV_CC1);
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k <= 16U; k++)
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.709s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t v = TIM_BM_ReadCapture(TIM17, 1U);
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        if (k != 0U)

[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +            s_t9_n++;
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        prev = v;
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +static void step_t9(void)
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    }

[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +                    (TIM_BM_ConfigInput(&ic) == TIM_BM_OK) && (TIM_BM_CaptureEnable(TIM17, TIM_BM_CH(1)) == TIM_BM_OK) &&

[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +/* A burst of n lines "TICK <label> <i> <HAL_GetTick>", one every
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   TICK_BURST_STEP_MS, each printed right after HAL_GetTick() reaches its
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   value (so the value is the start of that ms, not up to 1 ms old). Plan
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   R3-1: TICK_BURST_LONG lines at the banner and at the verdict, and
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   TICK_BURST_STEP lines at the start of every step, before its STEP line.
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   The host fits the means of the bursts (board ms, arrival time) by least
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   squares: the slope is the cross-check x, and u is the standard error of
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   the slope. The first line waits TICK_BURST_START_MS so the console has
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +   sent the lines before. The constants are next to step_begin(). */
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +static void tick_burst(const char *label, uint32_t n)
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = HAL_GetTick() + TICK_BURST_START_MS;
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t target = t0 + (i * TICK_BURST_STEP_MS);
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        while ((int32_t)(HAL_GetTick() - target) < 0)
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        LOG("TICK %s %lu %lu", label, (unsigned long)i, (unsigned long)HAL_GetTick());
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| + * Entry points
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| + * ========================================================================================== */

[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    __DSB();
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +static void copy_area_str(char *dst, const volatile char *src, uint32_t n)
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +        dst[i] = src[i];
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +    dst[n - 1U] = '\0';
[+   1.710s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +void tst_reset_image(int32_t kind, TstImg_t *value, TstImg_t *mask)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t present = tst_present(kind);
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c = kind_is_32(kind) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0xFFFFUL;   /* counter-wide registers */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < TST_RI_N; i++)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        value->v[i] = 0U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        mask->v[i]  = ((present & R(i)) != 0U) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    /* ARR resets to all ones: 0xFFFF (40.4.14, 42.4.12, 42.5.10, 43.5.12, 43.6.11, 44.4.8),

[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    value->v[TST_RI_ARR] = ((kind == TST_K_GP32) || (kind == TST_K_GP16)) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0xFFFFUL;

[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        mask->v[TST_RI_CNT] = c;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        mask->v[TST_RI_ARR] = c;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 13U; i <= 16U; i++)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            if ((present & R(i)) != 0U)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +                mask->v[i] = c;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    /* AF1 and AF2 reset to 0x0000 0001 (BKINE) on TIM1/TIM8 (40.4.26-29), AF1 on TIM15
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +       (43.5.19) and TIM16/17 (43.6.17, 43.6.19); TIM2-5 AF1 resets to 0 (41.4.22-25). */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if ((kind == TST_K_ADV) || (kind == TST_K_T15) || (kind == TST_K_T1617))
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        value->v[TST_RI_AF1] = 1U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (kind == TST_K_ADV)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        value->v[TST_RI_AF2] = 1U;

[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    const volatile uint32_t *r = (const volatile uint32_t *)tim;

[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        img->v[i] = ((i == TST_RI_EGR) || (i == TST_RI_DMAR) || (i == 20U)) ? 0U : r[i];
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +bool tst_reset_ok(const TIM_TypeDef *tim, uint32_t *bad_idx, uint32_t *got, uint32_t *exp)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    /* static: the selftest runs in one task per core, and its stack is small */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t img;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t value;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t mask;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    const int32_t kind = tst_kind(tim);
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (kind < 0)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    tst_img_read(tim, &img);
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    tst_reset_image(kind, &value, &mask);
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < TST_RI_N; i++)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        if (((img.v[i] ^ value.v[i]) & mask.v[i]) != 0U)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            *bad_idx = i;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            *got = img.v[i];
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            *exp = value.v[i] & mask.v[i];
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +            return false;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +/* Plan rule 2, typed again from RM0399 rev 4: ITR from tables 353 (TIM1/8), 358 (TIM2-5),
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +   361 (TIM12), 366 (TIM15); MMS from the CR2 sections; TISEL from 40.4.30-31 (TIM1
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +   TI1SEL 0001 = COMP1, TIM8 TI1SEL 0001 = COMP2: the plan's table says "ninguno", the RM
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +   rules), 41.4.26-29, 42.4.15, 42.5.12-13, 43.5.20, 43.6.18, 43.6.20. */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +bool tst_hand_caps(const TIM_TypeDef *tim, TimBm_Caps_t *c)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    const int32_t kind = tst_kind(tim);
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (kind < 0)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    *c = (TimBm_Caps_t){ 0 };
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    c->counter_bits = kind_is_32(kind) ? 32U : 16U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    switch (kind)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_ADV:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->channels = 4U; c->itr_mask = 0x000FU; c->mms_max = 7U; c->up_down_center = true; c->repetition = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->bdtr = true; c->slave_mode = true; c->master_mode = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x0003U; c->tisel_mask[1] = 0x0001U; c->tisel_mask[2] = 0x0001U; c->tisel_mask[3] = 0x0001U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_GP32:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_GP16:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->channels = 4U; c->mms_max = 7U; c->up_down_center = true; c->slave_mode = true; c->master_mode = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x0001U; c->tisel_mask[1] = 0x0001U; c->tisel_mask[2] = 0x0001U; c->tisel_mask[3] = 0x0001U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        if (tim == TIM2)      { c->itr_mask = 0x007FU; c->tisel_mask[3] = 0x000FU; }   /* ITR0-6; TI4: COMP1/2 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        else if (tim == TIM3) { c->itr_mask = 0x001FU; c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x000FU; }   /* ITR0-4; TI1: COMP1/2 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        else if (tim == TIM4) { c->itr_mask = 0x000FU; }                               /* ITR0-3               */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        else                  { c->itr_mask = 0x01CFU; c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x0007U; }   /* TIM5: ITR0-3, 6-8; TI1: fdcan1 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_BASIC:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->mms_max = 2U; c->master_mode = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_T12:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->channels = 2U; c->itr_mask = 0x000FU; c->mms_max = 5U; c->slave_mode = true; c->master_mode = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x0003U; c->tisel_mask[1] = 0x0001U;                         /* TI1: spdifrx */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_T1314:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->channels = 1U; c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x0001U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    case TST_K_T15:
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->channels = 2U; c->itr_mask = 0x000FU; c->mms_max = 5U; c->repetition = true; c->bdtr = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->slave_mode = true; c->master_mode = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->tisel_mask[0] = 0x007FU; c->tisel_mask[1] = 0x000FU;                         /* TI1: 1-6, TI2: 1-3 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    default:   /* TST_K_T1617 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->channels = 1U; c->repetition = true; c->bdtr = true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        c->tisel_mask[0] = (tim == TIM16) ? 0x000FU : 0x000DU;                           /* TIM17: 0, 2, 3 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +        break;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T2: the field encodings, by hand ------------------------------------------- */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +/* CR1 (40.4.1): URS bit 2, OPM bit 3, DIR bit 4, CMS bits 6:5, ARPE bit 7. */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t enc_cr1(const TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t *tb)
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t v = 0U;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (tb->urs_overflow_only) { v |= 1UL << 2; }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (tb->one_pulse)         { v |= 1UL << 3; }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (tb->count_mode == TIM_BM_COUNT_DOWN) { v |= 1UL << 4; }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (tb->count_mode >= TIM_BM_COUNT_CENTER1) { v |= (tb->count_mode - 1U) << 5; }   /* CENTER1 -> 01 */
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    if (tb->arr_preload)       { v |= 1UL << 7; }
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +    return v;
[+   1.713s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +/* CCMR1/CCMR2 (40.4.7, 40.4.9): channel n in the byte (n-1)%2 of CCMR1 (n = 1, 2) or
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +   CCMR2 (n = 3, 4). Output: CCxS 1:0 = 00, OCxPE bit 3, OCxM bits 6:4. Input: CCxS
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +   1:0, ICxPSC 3:2, ICxF 7:4. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t ccmr_word(uint32_t ch)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    return (ch <= 2U) ? TST_RI_CCMR1 : TST_RI_CCMR2;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t ccmr_sh(uint32_t ch)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    return ((ch - 1U) % 2U) * 8U;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t psc_bits(uint32_t prescaler)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    return (prescaler == 8U) ? 3U : ((prescaler == 4U) ? 2U : ((prescaler == 2U) ? 1U : 0U));
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +/* TS[4:0] (41.4.3): ITR0-3 = 00000-00011, TI1F_ED 00100, TI1FP1 00101, TI2FP2 00110,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +   ITR4-8 = 01000-01100; TS[2:0] in SMCR bits 6:4, TS[4:3] in bits 21:20. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t enc_ts(uint32_t trg)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static const uint8_t k_ts[19] = { 0U, 1U, 2U, 3U, 8U, 9U, 10U, 11U, 12U,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                                      0xFFU, 0xFFU, 0xFFU, 0xFFU, 0xFFU, 0xFFU, 0xFFU,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                                      4U, 5U, 6U };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t ts = k_ts[trg];
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    return ((ts & 7U) << 4) | ((ts >> 3) << 20);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +/* SMS[3:0] (40.4.3): SMS[2:0] bits 2:0, SMS[3] bit 16. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t enc_sms(uint32_t sms)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    return (sms & 7U) | ((sms >> 3) << 16);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +static void t2_check(TIM_TypeDef *tim, const TstT2Cfg_t *cfg, TimBm_Status_t st, const TstImg_t *exp,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                     const TstImg_t *mask, TstT2Hook_t hook, TstT2Result_t *res)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t got;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    tst_img_read(tim, &got);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    res->configs++;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    res->per_class[cfg->cls]++;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    if (st != TIM_BM_OK)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        res->calls_bad++;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < TST_RI_N; i++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        if ((i != TST_RI_CNT) && (((got.v[i] ^ exp->v[i]) & mask->v[i]) != 0U))
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            if (res->mismatches == 0U)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                res->first_cfg = res->configs - 1U;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                res->first_idx = i;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                res->first_got = got.v[i];
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                res->first_exp = exp->v[i] & mask->v[i];
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                res->first_cls = cfg->cls;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            res->mismatches++;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    if (hook != NULL)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        hook(cfg, &got);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +void tst_t2_run(TIM_TypeDef *tim, TstT2Hook_t hook, TstT2Result_t *res)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_sms[6] = { TIM_BM_SMS_DISABLED, TIM_BM_SMS_RESET, TIM_BM_SMS_GATED, TIM_BM_SMS_TRIGGER,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                                       TIM_BM_SMS_EXT_CLOCK1, TIM_BM_SMS_RESET_TRIGGER };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_icpsc[4] = { 1U, 2U, 4U, 8U };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_icf[3] = { 0U, 7U, 15U };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t k_edge[3] = { TIM_BM_EDGE_RISING, TIM_BM_EDGE_FALLING, TIM_BM_EDGE_BOTH };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    TimBm_Caps_t caps;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t reset;     /* static: a small task stack on both cores */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t mask;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t exp;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static TstImg_t mk;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    static TstT2Cfg_t cfg;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    *res = (TstT2Result_t){ 0 };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    const int32_t kind = tst_kind(tim);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    if ((kind < 0) || !tst_hand_caps(tim, &caps))
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        res->calls_bad = 1U;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    tst_reset_image(kind, &reset, &mask);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cmax = (caps.counter_bits == 32U) ? 0xFFFFFFFFUL : 0xFFFFUL;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    /* Time base: every count mode, ARPE, OPM, URS. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t n_modes = caps.up_down_center ? 5U : 1U;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < n_modes; m++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 8U; b++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            cfg = (TstT2Cfg_t){ 0 };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            cfg.cls = TST_T2_TB;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            cfg.tb = (TimBm_TimeBaseConfig_t){ tim, 7U, (caps.counter_bits == 32U) ? 0x12345UL : 999UL, m,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                                               caps.repetition ? 3U : 0U, (b & 1U) != 0U, (b & 2U) != 0U,
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                                               (b & 4U) != 0U };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(tim);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            const TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_InitTimeBase(&cfg.tb);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            exp = reset;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            exp.v[TST_RI_CR1] = enc_cr1(&cfg.tb);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            exp.v[TST_RI_PSC] = cfg.tb.psc;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            exp.v[TST_RI_ARR] = cfg.tb.arr;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            exp.v[TST_RI_RCR] = cfg.tb.repetition;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            mk = mask;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            if (m >= TIM_BM_COUNT_CENTER1)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                mk.v[TST_RI_CR1] &= ~(1UL << 4);    /* DIR: read-only status in center mode (40.4.1) */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            if ((m == 0U) && (b == 0U))
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                /* The control: an expected image altered on purpose must be flagged. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t cr1 = tim->CR1;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                exp.v[TST_RI_CR1] ^= 1UL << 7;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                res->ctrl_detected = (((cr1 ^ exp.v[TST_RI_CR1]) & mk.v[TST_RI_CR1]) != 0U) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                exp.v[TST_RI_CR1] ^= 1UL << 7;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            t2_check(tim, &cfg, st, &exp, &mk, hook, res);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    /* Output compare: 8 modes x 2 polarities x preload, every channel. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t ch = 1U; ch <= caps.channels; ch++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t mode = 0U; mode < 8U; mode++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 4U; b++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                cfg = (TstT2Cfg_t){ 0 };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                cfg.cls = TST_T2_OC;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                cfg.oc = (TimBm_OcConfig_t){ tim, ch, mode, 0x1234U & cmax, (b & 1U) != 0U, (b & 2U) != 0U };
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                (void)TIM_BM_Deinit(tim);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                const TimBm_Status_t st = TIM_BM_ConfigOutput(&cfg.oc);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                exp = reset;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                exp.v[ccmr_word(ch)] = ((mode << 4) | (cfg.oc.preload ? (1UL << 3) : 0U)) << ccmr_sh(ch);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                exp.v[TST_RI_CCR1 + ch - 1U] = cfg.oc.compare;
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                exp.v[TST_RI_CCER] = cfg.oc.active_low ? (1UL << (4U * (ch - 1U) + 1U)) : 0U;   /* CCxP */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                t2_check(tim, &cfg, st, &exp, &mask, hook, res);
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    /* Input capture: 3 selections x 3 edges x 4 prescalers x filters 0/7/15 x valid TISEL. */
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t ch = 1U; ch <= caps.channels; ch++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t sel = TIM_BM_IC_DIRECT; sel <= TIM_BM_IC_TRC; sel++)
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            if (((sel == TIM_BM_IC_INDIRECT) && (caps.channels < 2U)) || ((sel == TIM_BM_IC_TRC) && !caps.slave_mode))
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.714s] [host] diff| +                continue;
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t e = 0U; e < 3U; e++)
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t p = 0U; p < 4U; p++)
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +                    for (uint32_t f = 0U; f < 3U; f++)
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +                        for (uint32_t ts = 0U; ts < 16U; ts++)
[+   1.715s] [host] diff| +                        {

[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +def t3_calc_time_base(clk: int, target: int, arr_max: int) -> tuple:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    """The plan's rule, with Fractions: (status, psc, arr, ticks, error_ppm)."""
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if target == 0 or target > clk // 2 or arr_max == 0:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_PARAM, 0, 0, 0, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    psc = max(math.ceil(Fraction(clk, target * (arr_max + 1))) - 1, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if psc > 65535:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_RANGE, 0, 0, 0, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    n = math.floor(Fraction(clk, (psc + 1) * target) + Fraction(1, 2))     # half rounds up
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    n = min(n, arr_max + 1)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if n < 2:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_RANGE, 0, 0, 0, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    ticks = (psc + 1) * n
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    num = clk * 10**6 // ticks - target * 10**6                            # both >= 0 terms: floor = trunc
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    return (ST_OK, psc, n - 1, ticks, int(Fraction(num, target)))          # int(): toward zero
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +def t3_duty(arr: int, bits: int, duty: int) -> tuple:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if bits not in (16, 32) or duty > 10000:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_PARAM, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    cmax = (1 << bits) - 1
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if arr > cmax:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_PARAM, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if duty == 10000 and arr + 1 > cmax:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_RANGE, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    return (ST_OK, math.floor(Fraction((arr + 1) * duty, 10000) + Fraction(1, 2)))
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +def t3_kernel(hclk: int, d2ppre: int, timpre: int) -> tuple:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if d2ppre > 7 or timpre > 1:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        return (ST_PARAM, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    return (ST_OK, hclk // TABLE59_DIV[timpre][d2ppre])

[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +def t3_min_error_info(clk: int, target: int, arr_max: int, psc0: int, span: int = 4096) -> tuple[int, int]:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    """Information only (decision 3): the smallest |error| over psc0..psc0+span with the
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    best n for each psc, against the plan's rule. Returns (best |ppm|, its psc)."""
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    best, best_psc = None, psc0
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    for psc in range(psc0, min(65535, psc0 + span) + 1):
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        d = (psc + 1) * target
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        for n in (clk // d, clk // d + 1):
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            if 2 <= n <= arr_max + 1:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +                e = abs(Fraction(clk, (psc + 1) * n) - target) / target * 10**6
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +                if best is None or e < best:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +                    best, best_psc = e, psc
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    return (int(best) if best is not None else -1, best_psc)

[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +def t3_check(lines: list[str], log: Callable[[str], None]) -> list[tuple[str, bool, str]]:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    """Recomputes every T3 vector; returns (check name, ok, detail) entries."""
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    ct, dc, kc = [], [], []
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    for line in lines:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        for rx, dst in ((T3_CT_RX, ct), (T3_DC_RX, dc), (T3_KC_RX, kc)):
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            m = rx.search(line)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            if m:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +                dst.append(m.groups())
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    bad_ct, bad_dc, bad_kc = [], [], []
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    worse = []
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    first_ok = None
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    for g in ct:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        i, clk, target, arr_max, st, psc, arr, ticks_hex, err, _cyc = g
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        clk, target, arr_max = int(clk), int(target), int(arr_max)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        got = (int(st), int(psc), int(arr), int(ticks_hex, 16), int(err))
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        exp = t3_calc_time_base(clk, target, arr_max)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        if exp[0] != ST_OK:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            got = (got[0], 0, 0, 0, 0)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        if got != exp:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            bad_ct.append((int(i), got, exp))
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        elif exp[0] == ST_OK:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            if first_ok is None:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +                first_ok = (got, exp)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +            if len(worse) < 12 and exp[4] != 0:

[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +def t6_expect(p: tuple) -> tuple[int, int]:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    """(period, high) in timer ticks of a T6 point on the OCx pin."""
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    psc, arr, mode, ccr, low, center = p
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if center:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        per = 2 * arr * (psc + 1)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        high = min(2 * ccr, 2 * arr) * (psc + 1)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    else:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        per = (psc + 1) * (arr + 1)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        high = min(ccr, arr + 1) * (psc + 1)
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if mode == OC_PWM2:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        high = per - high
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    if low:
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +        high = per - high
[+   1.716s] [host] diff| +    return per, high

[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +    def _dout_start(self, ch: int, f: float, duty: int) -> tuple[float, float]:
[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +        """Digital Out pulse on DIO1/DIO3 through the S11 guard. Returns (f_dout, high_s)."""
[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +        clk = self.ad2.dout_clock_hz()
[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +        for div in (1, 10, 100):
[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +            total = int(round(clk / div / f))
[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +            high = total * duty // 100

[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +            except ad2mod.AD2Error:

[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +            time.sleep(0.001)                                          # S6

[+   1.718s] [host] diff| +            return f_cfg, high * div / clk
